\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\sf{(tr)}}\|^{p}\Big]=\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\prod_{s=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}\,\{\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\}\Big\|^{p}\Big]=\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\Big(\frac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\Gamma_{t}^{c}\Big)\prod_{s=1}^{t-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}\{\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\,.

Applying Minkowski’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\sf{(tr)}}\|^{p}]\leq\frac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\Gamma_{t}^{c}\cdot\prod_{s=1}^{t-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}\{\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\,.

By using
1
conditionally, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\sf{(tr)}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t}a)^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\prod_{s=1}^{t-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}\{\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\leq\prod_{s=1}^{t}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{H_{s}}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|\,,

which gives the following bound on the transient term

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\sf{(tr)}}\|^{p}]\leq\exp{\Big(-a\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}H_{s}\Big)}\,\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|\,.

∎

Bound on the fluctuation term.

Lemma 7
.

Assume
1
(p),
2
, and let
\eta_{s}\leq\eta_{\infty,p}
. Then, it holds

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\sf{(fl)}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\,N^{-1}p^{2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{2}(\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\wedge\frac{\eta_{s}}{a})\right)^{1/2}\,.

Proof.

We now bound the fluctuation term
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}\|^{p}]
.
By definition of
\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}\|^{p}]=\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\Big\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\,.

Notice that the
\varphi^{\sf avg}_{t}
are of zero expectation, and independent of each other. Thus,
\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\}
forms a martingale difference w.r.t the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{i+1,1}^{+}
.
Using Burkholder’s inequality (
40
, Theorem 8.6), we then obtain the following bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}\|^{p}]\leq p\left(\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\Big\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\|^{2}\Big)^{p/2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

Using Minkowski’s inequality along with
1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\cdot\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\right)^{1/2}\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)\,\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]}\right)^{1/2}\,.

Now we have to estimate
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]
.
Recall that
\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\eta_{s}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z^{c}_{s,h})
.
Therefore, applying Burkholder’s inequality and Minkowski’s inequality again gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p}{N}\left(\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\eta_{s}^{2}\|\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h}^{c})\|^{2}\Big)^{p/2}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p}{N}\left(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h}^{c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}\,.

Using
1
and the definition of
\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\varphi^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]\leq\frac{p\eta_{s}}{N}\left(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s})^{2(H_{s}-h)}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{2}\right)^{1/2}\,\leq pN^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\sqrt{(H_{s}\wedge\frac{1}{\eta_{s}a})}\,,

where
\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s})^{2(H_{s}-h)}
is bounded by
H_{s}\wedge\frac{1}{\eta_{s}a}
exactly as in
Lemma

4
.
Finally, we bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\,N^{-1}p^{2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{2}(\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\wedge\frac{\eta_{s}}{a})\right)^{1/2}\,.

∎

Bound on the bias.

The bias term,
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(bi, bi)}}\|^{p}]
, is deterministic, it’s
p
-th moment coincides with
\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(bi, bi)}}\|
, and it follows from
Lemma

3
, which states

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(bi, bi)}}\|^{p}]=\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(bi, bi)}}\|\leq\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}^{2}\exp{\Big(-a\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\,.

Bound on the fluctuations of the bias.

Lemma 8
.

Assume
1
(p),
2
, and let
\eta_{s}\leq\eta_{\infty,p}
. Then, it holds

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\sf{(fl,bi)}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}p^{2}N^{-1/2}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p^{3}\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{Na\mathrm{e}}}\sqrt{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{5}H_{s}^{4}\exp{\Big(-a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}}\,.

Proof.

Recall the definition of
\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl, bi)}}
from (
10
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl, bi)}}=\underbrace{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}}_{\bm{\mathrm{T_{1}}}}+\underbrace{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\left\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\mathbb{E}\Big[\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma_{i}^{\textrm{avg}}\Big]\right\}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}}_{\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}}\,.

Bound on
\bm{\mathrm{T_{1}}}
.

We start by deriving a
p-
th moment of
\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{c=1}^{N}(G_{s}^{c}-\Gamma_{s}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})
.
Since the clients have independent noise, we can apply Burkholder’s inequality to bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]\leq\frac{1}{N}\Big(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(G_{s}^{c}-\Gamma_{s}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\|^{p}]}_{\bm{\mathrm{U_{c,p}^{2}}}}\Big)^{1/2}\,.

Now we use
Lemma

22
to estimate
\bm{\mathrm{U_{c,p}}}
:

\displaystyle\bm{\mathrm{U_{c,p}}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\Big(\prod_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{s}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})-\prod_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{s}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h}^{c}))\Big)(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\Big\|^{p}\Big]

\displaystyle=\eta_{s}\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{s}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}(\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}-\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h}^{c}))\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\Big\|^{p}\Big]\,.

As
\mathbb{E}[(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{s}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}(\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h}^{c})-\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}|\mathcal{F}_{s,h+1}^{+}]=0
, we can apply Burkholder’s inequality here

\displaystyle\bm{\mathrm{U_{c,p}}}

\displaystyle\leq p\eta_{s}\left(\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big\|(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{s}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}(\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}-\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h}^{c}))\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\Big\|^{p}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq p\eta_{s}\left(\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(h-1)}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(H_{s}-h)}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=p\eta_{s}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\left(\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(H_{s}-1)}\right)^{1/2}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|=p\eta_{s}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{H_{s}-1}H_{s}^{1/2}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}pN^{-1/2}\eta_{s}H_{s}^{1/2}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{H_{s}-1}\frac{\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}}{N}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}pN^{-1/2}\eta_{s}H_{s}^{1/2}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{H_{s}-1}\,.

Now we are ready to estimate
\bm{\mathrm{T_{1}}}
. We use here that
\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\tau_{s}\}_{s=1}^{t}
forms a martingale difference, therefore we apply Burkholder’s inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\bm{\mathrm{T_{1}}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\,\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{2H_{i}}\,\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}=2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}p^{2}N^{-1/2}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s}^{t}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{2H_{i}}\,\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\right)^{1/2}

We can rewrite this estimate as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\bm{\mathrm{T_{1}}}\|^{p}]\leq 2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}p^{2}N^{-1/2}\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\right)^{1/2}

Bound on
T_{2}
.

Now we estimate
p-
th moment of
\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}
, where we recall that
\Delta_{s,t}=\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\}-\mathbb{E}[\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}]
. We start from single summand
\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}

\displaystyle\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}

\displaystyle=\Big(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G_{i}^{\textrm{avg}}\Big)\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}=\Big(\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\Big\{\!\!\prod_{r=i+1}^{t}\!\!\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{r}\Big\}\cdot\Big\{\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\textrm{avg}}_{i}\Big\}\cdot\Big\{\!\!\prod_{r=s+1}^{i-1}\!\!G^{\textrm{avg}}_{r}\Big\}\Big)\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\,.

We first bound moment of
(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\textrm{avg}}_{i})u
for an arbitrary vector
u\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
i\geq 0
.
By applying Burkholder’s inequality

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\|(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\textrm{avg}}_{i})u\|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big\|\frac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}(\Gamma_{i,1:H_{i}}^{c}-G_{i,1:H_{i}}^{c})u\Big\|^{p}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{p}{N}\Big)^{p}\left(\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\|(\Gamma_{i,1:H_{i}}^{c}-G_{i,1:H_{i}}^{c})u\|^{2}\Big)^{p/2}\Big]\right)^{p/2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{p}{N}\Big)^{p}\left(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(\Gamma_{i,1:H_{i}}^{c}-G_{i,1:H_{i}}^{c})u\|^{p}]\right)^{p/2}\,.

Using
Lemma

22
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\|(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\textrm{avg}}_{i})u\|^{p}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{p\eta_{i}}{N}\Big)^{p}\left(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big\|\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{i}}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{i}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{i,h}^{c})\Gamma_{i,h+1:H_{i}}^{c}u\Big\|^{p}\Big]\right)^{p/2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{p\eta_{i}}{N}\Big)^{p}\left(\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{i}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big\|(I-\eta_{i}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{i,h}^{c})\Gamma_{i,h+1:H_{i}}^{c}u\Big\|^{p}\Big]\right)^{p/2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{p\eta_{i}}{N}\Big)^{p}\left(NH_{i}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{2(h-1)}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{2(H_{i}-h)}\right)^{p/2}\,,

or, in other words,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\sf avg}_{i})u\|^{p}]\leq\frac{2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p\eta_{i}\sqrt{H_{i}}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{H_{i}}\|u\|}{\sqrt{N}}\,.

Now we can bound
\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{1/p}\Big[\Big\|\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\{\prod_{r=i+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{r}\}\cdot(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\textrm{avg}}_{i})\cdot\{\prod_{r=s+1}^{i-1}G^{\textrm{avg}}_{r}\}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\|^{p}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}\Big[\Big\|\{\prod_{r=i+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{r}\}\cdot(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-G^{\textrm{avg}}_{i})\cdot\{\prod_{r=s+1}^{i-1}G^{\textrm{avg}}_{r}\}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\Big\|^{p}\Big]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\prod_{r=s+1,r\neq i}^{t}(1-\eta_{r}a)^{2H_{r}}\frac{4\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}p^{2}\eta_{i}^{2}H_{i}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{2H_{i}}\|\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{2}}{N}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p^{2}\|\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\prod_{r=s+1}^{t}(1-\eta_{r}a)^{2H_{r}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}^{2}H_{i}\right)^{1/2}

As
\eta_{i}\geq\eta_{s}
for all
i>s
, we can rewrite the bound as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}\|^{p}]\leq\frac{2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p^{2}\|\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|}{\sqrt{N}}\eta_{s}^{1/2}\exp{\Big(-a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\sqrt{\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}}

Now, finally, we can bound
\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}
.
Remark that
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}\|^{p}]\leq p\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\Delta_{s,t}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}
, and thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\frac{4\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}p^{4}\|\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{2}}{N}\eta_{s}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p^{3}}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\|\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{2}\eta_{s}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p^{3}\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{N}}\sqrt{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{5}H_{s}^{4}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}}\,.

Then we use the fact that
x\exp{(-2cx)}\leq 1/(ec)\exp{(-cx)}
for
c>0
and
x\geq 0
and get:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}\|^{p}]\leq\frac{\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}p^{3}\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}}{\sqrt{Na\mathrm{e}}}\sqrt{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{5}H_{s}^{4}\exp{\Big(-a\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\Big)}}\,.

Combining
\bm{\mathrm{T_{1}}}
and
\bm{\mathrm{T_{2}}}
finishes the proof.
∎

Convergence result.

Now, we are ready to state the main result for the moment bounds
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}\|^{p}]
.

A.3.1
Proof of the
Theorem

2

Proof.

We start from the decomposition of the last iterate (
37
). Then, by applying Minkowski’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}\|^{p}]\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{tr}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(bi, bi)}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl, bi)}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\tilde{\theta}_{t}^{\text{(fl)}}\|^{p}]

By controlling each of the terms by using
Lemma

3
,
Lemma

6
,
Lemma

7
and
Lemma

8
we get the desired result.
∎

Note that the bound in
Theorem

2
can be rewritten in a slightly simpler form.
Indeed, the last two terms in the bound can be controlled by
(1+t)^{-\gamma}
up to a multiplicative constant.
This simplified representation will be useful in the Gaussian approximation analysis for the multiplier bootstrap.

Corollary 5
.

Under the assumptions of
Theorem

2
and
2
, for any
t\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}\|^{p}]\leq\exp{\left(-a\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}H_{s}\right)}\,\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|+\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},1}p^{3}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

(54)

where
\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},1}
and
\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},2}
are defined in (
A.3.1
).

Proof.

We introduce the constants

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},1}

\displaystyle=(2\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}+4(a\mathrm{e})^{-1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2})((\eta H)^{1/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{-1/2}(1+(a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\,,

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},2}

\displaystyle=\zeta_{1}\zeta_{2}(\eta^{1/2}H+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(1+(a\eta H)^{\gamma\over 1-\gamma}))\,.

(55)

Applying
Lemma

25
, we obtain the bound (
54
).
∎

Appendix B
Normal approximation for FedLSA

In this section, we provide the proof of

Theorem

3
, which establishes a Berry–Esseen type bound
for the last iterate of
FedLSA
.

For notational simplicity, we slightly abuse notation and write

Z_{i,j,c}:=Z_{i,j}^{c}
and
\theta_{i,j,c}:=\theta_{i,j}^{c}
.

B.1
Error decomposition and linear statistics for Gaussian approximation

Firstly, for any
t\in\{1,\dots,T\}
and
h\in\{1,\dots,H\}
, we unroll the recursion in (
30
), as follows

\displaystyle\theta_{t,h}^{c}-\theta^{c}_{\star}

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c}))(\theta_{t,h-1}^{c}-\theta^{c}_{\star})-\eta_{t}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})

(56)

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{t,h}^{c}(\theta_{t,0}^{c}-\theta^{c}_{\star})-\eta_{t}\sum_{j=1}^{h}\Gamma_{t,j+1:h}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{t,j,c})\,,

(57)

where for any
j\geq 1
and
\theta\in\mathbb{R}^{d},z\in\mathsf{Z}
, we recall the definition of the following values

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{c}(\theta,z)=\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(z)\theta-\widetilde{\mathbf{b}}^{c}(z)\,,

\displaystyle G_{t,\ell:r}^{c}=(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{r-\ell+1},\quad\ell\leq r\,,

\displaystyle G_{t,\ell:r}^{c}=\mathrm{I},\quad\ell>r\,,

\displaystyle G_{t,j}^{c}=G_{t,1:j}^{c}\,.

Also, we set the following quantities

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{t,h}=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\Gamma_{t,1:h}^{c}\,,\quad G^{\sf avg}_{t,h}=N^{-1}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}G_{t,h}^{c}\,.

Now, we note that

\displaystyle N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\{\theta_{t,h}^{c}-\theta_{\star}\}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{t,h}(\theta_{t-1}-\theta_{\star})+\Delta^{\sf avg}_{t,h}-\eta_{t}\mathcal{E}^{\sf avg}_{t,h}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{t,h}\prod_{i=1}^{t-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})+\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{t,h}\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}-\eta_{s}\mathcal{E}^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}})+\Delta^{\sf avg}_{t,h}-\eta_{t}\mathcal{E}^{\sf avg}_{t,h}\,,

where we have set, for any
h\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\Delta^{\sf avg}_{t,h}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}(\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{t,h}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\,,\quad\text{and }\quad\mathcal{E}^{\sf avg}_{t,h}=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{s=1}^{h}\Gamma_{t,s+1:h}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{t,s,c})\,.

Particularly, for
h=H_{t}
, we obtain the last iterate decomposition, that is,

\displaystyle\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}=\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}-\eta_{s}\mathcal{E}^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}})\,.

In this decomposition, the dominant term is the sum of the noise variables
\mathcal{E}^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}
.
We isolate it in the decomposition

\displaystyle\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-N^{-1}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})}_{\text{Linear statistics}}+\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})+\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}

\displaystyle-N^{-1}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\left(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\right)\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})

(58)

\displaystyle-N^{-1}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}-G_{s,h+1:H_{s}})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})

However, the underbraced term is not the complete leading linear term. To get the correct leading term we have to extract the hidden from the first glance linear statistics from the summand
\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}
. Recalling the definitions from (
34
), we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}=\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}+\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\,.

Here,
\rho^{\sf avg}_{t}=\frac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}(\mathrm{I}-G_{s}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})
represents the deterministic bias and does not contain any random noise, while
\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}=\frac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}(G_{s}^{c}-\Gamma^{c}_{t,1:H_{s}})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})
contains linear noise from the
(G_{s}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,1:H_{s}}^{c})
. Therefore, to extract full linear statistics from
\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}
we need to linearize
\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}
.

Linearization of
\bm{\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}}
.

We need the following linearization to establish the correct leading term. Recalling the definitions from (
34
), we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s,H_{s}}=\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}+\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}\,.

We aim to linearize the
\tau^{\sf avg}_{s}
term in order to extract the leading linear statistic.
To this end, we linearize terms of the form

\displaystyle G_{s}^{c}\!-\!\Gamma_{s}^{c}

\displaystyle=\eta_{s}\sum_{i=1}^{H_{s}}G_{s,1:i-1}^{c}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,i,c})\Gamma_{s,i+1:H_{s}}^{c}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\eta_{s}\sum_{i=1}^{H_{s}}G_{s,1:i-1}^{c}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,i,c})}_{U_{s}^{c}}+\underbrace{\eta_{s}\sum_{i=1}^{H_{s}}G_{s,1:i-1}^{c}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,i,c})(\Gamma_{s,i+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\mathrm{I})}_{R_{s}^{c}}\,.

(59)

First, we bound the term
U_{s}^{c}
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|U_{s}^{c}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(i-1)}{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}\leq\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\,.

Then, we write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|R_{s}^{c}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(i-1)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\mathbb{E}[\|\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,i+1:H_{s}}^{c}\|^{2}]\,.

Next, we bound
\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,i+1:H_{s}}^{c}\|^{2}]\leq\sum_{j=i+1}^{H_{s}}\eta_{s}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{j-i-1}\leq\eta_{s}H_{s}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}
.Thus, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|R_{s}^{c}\|^{2}]\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{3}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,.

(60)

Leading linear statistics.

The correct leading linear statistics defined in (
58
) consists of two terms

\displaystyle M_{t}

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})

(61)

\displaystyle\quad+N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}G_{s,1:h-1}^{c}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\,.

(62)

We can decompose
M_{t}
in four terms,

\displaystyle M_{t}

\displaystyle=M_{t,1}+M_{t,2}+M_{t,3}+M_{t,4}\,,

where

\displaystyle M_{t,1}

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,,

\displaystyle M_{t,2}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\,,

\displaystyle M_{t,3}

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\mathrm{I})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,,

\displaystyle M_{t,4}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(G_{s,1:h-1}^{c}-\mathrm{I})\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\,.

From the definition (
3
) of
\varepsilon^{c}
, we see that the first and second terms can be combined into

\displaystyle\hat{M}_{t}=M_{t,1}+M_{t,2}=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})\,.

Let us denote

\displaystyle\Sigma^{c}_{*}=\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z)(\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z))^{T}]\,,\quad\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\Sigma^{c}_{*}\,.

(63)

Then, we define the covariance matrix, corresponding to the term
M_{t,1}+M_{t,2}
, as

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\hat{M}_{t}\hat{M}_{t}^{T}]=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\Sigma_{*}\left(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\right)^{T}\,,

(64)

which is the leading component of the FedLSA covariance. According to
Lemma

13
, the terms
M_{t,3}
and
M_{t,4}
are residual.

We may notice that in the case when
H=\text{const}
, the leading component of FedLSA covariance
\hat{\Sigma}_{t}
coincides with that of
5
.

Final error decomposition for Gaussian approximation.

We now define

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[M_{t}M_{t}^{T}]\,,

(65)

which allows us to write the central error decomposition for the normalized statistic
\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star})

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star})=W_{t}+D_{t}\,,

(66)

where we define

\displaystyle W_{t}

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}M_{t}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\upsilon_{s,h,c}\,,

(67)

\displaystyle D_{t}

\displaystyle=D_{t,1}+D_{t,2}+D_{t,3}+D_{t,4}+D_{t,5}+D_{t,6}\,,

(68)

with

\displaystyle\upsilon_{s,h,c}=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\left\{G_{s,1:h-1}^{c}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})-G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\right\}\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,1}=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,2}=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}R_{s}^{c}(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,3}=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\Big(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\Big)\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,4}=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\rho^{\sf avg}_{s}\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,5}=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\Big(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\Big)\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,6}=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,.

Theorem
3

Now we are ready to proof
Theorem

3
– the main result of this section.

B.2
Proof of the Theorem
3

Applying
47
to the decomposition (
66
), we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}),Y)\leq\underbrace{259d^{1/2}\Upsilon_{t}}_{\text{$R_{1}$}}+\underbrace{2\mathbb{E}[\|W_{t}\|\|D_{t}\|]}_{\text{$R_{2}$}}+\underbrace{2\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|\|D_{t}-D_{t}^{(s,h,c)}\|]}_{\text{$R_{3}$}}\,,

(69)

where
\Upsilon_{t}=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|^{3}]
and
D_{t}^{(s,h,c)}
is a counterpart of
D_{t}
, in which we have replaced
Z_{s,h,c}
by its independent copy
Z_{s,h,c}^{\prime}
. In the same way, we define
D_{t,i}^{(s,h,c)}
for
i\in\{1,2,3,4\}
.

Thus, to obtain the desired result we have to bound
R_{1}
,
R_{2}
and
R_{3}
.

Bound on
R_{1}

At first, we note that

\displaystyle\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}(1-\eta_{i}a)^{H_{i}}((1-\eta_{s}a)^{H_{s}-h}\|\varepsilon\|_{\infty}+(1-\eta_{s}a)^{h-1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|)

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}((1-\eta_{s}a)^{H_{s}-h}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+(1-\eta_{s}a)^{h-1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|)

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|)\,.

Thus, applying
Lemma

25
, we get

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{t}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|^{3}]\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{3}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-3/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}\exp{\left(-3a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})^{3}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\eta^{2}H+La^{-1}(1+(3a\eta H)^{2\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma\over 1-\gamma}))\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{3}N^{-1/2}\eta^{1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})^{3}\,,

(70)

where we have set

\displaystyle\zeta_{3}^{2}=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\,.

Bound on
R_{2}

Firstly, we establish the bound on
\Delta^{\sf avg}_{j}
for any
1\leq j\leq t
. By the definition of
\Delta^{\sf avg}_{j}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta^{\sf avg}_{j}=N^{-1}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}(\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{j,H_{j}}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})=\tau^{\sf avg}_{j}+\rho^{\sf avg}_{j}\,.

Then, using
Lemma

2
and (
50
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{j}\|^{2}]\leq 2\mathbb{E}[\|\tau^{\sf avg}_{j}\|^{2}]+2\mathbb{E}[\|\rho^{\sf avg}_{j}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{het}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{j}^{2}H_{j}+\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}\eta_{j}^{4}H_{j}^{4}

\displaystyle\,.

(71)

Now, Applying Hölders inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|W_{t}\|\|D_{t}\|]\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|W_{t}\|^{2}]\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t}\|^{2}]\,.

(72)

Firstly, we note that
\mathbb{E}[\|W_{t}\|^{2}]=d
. Therefore, we can proceed with the
D_{t}
. For
D_{t,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}^{(1-\gamma)^{-1}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\exp{(-a(1-\gamma)^{-1}\eta H(1+t)^{1-\gamma})}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}\exp{\left(-{a\over 2(1-\gamma)}\eta H(1+t)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|\,,

(73)

where we have used that
x^{1/2}e^{-cx^{1-\gamma}}\leq(c\mathrm{e}(1-\gamma))^{-{1\over 2(1-\gamma)}}e^{-cx^{1-\gamma}/2}
for any
x,c>0
and have set
\operatorname{c}_{D,1}=2\mathrm{e}^{(1-\gamma)^{-1}}(a\mathrm{e}\eta H)^{-{1\over 2(1-\gamma)}}\eta^{1/2}
.
Using the martingale-difference structure of the sequence
\{R_{s}^{c},1\leq s\leq t\}
for any
c
, (
60
) and applying
Lemma

25
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,2}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\eta H\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\gamma}\right)}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,2}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}\,,

(74)

where

\operatorname{c}_{D,2}^{2}=8a^{-1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\zeta_{4}^{2}((\eta H)^{3}+La^{-1}(\eta H)^{2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{3\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))\,

and we have set

\displaystyle\zeta_{4}^{2}={1\over N}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\,.

Then, again using the martingale-difference structure of the sequence
\{\Big(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\Big)\tau^{\sf avg}_{s},1\leq s\leq t\}
,
Lemma

16
and (
B.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,3}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}\|^{2}]\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 4a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\zeta_{3}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle+2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{5}H_{s}^{4}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,3,1}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}}+\operatorname{c}_{D,3,2}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}(1+t)^{-3\gamma}\,.

(75)

where

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{D,3,1}^{2}

\displaystyle=64a^{-2}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\zeta_{3}^{2}(\eta^{2}H+La^{-1}\eta(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+2\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))\,,

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{D,3,2}^{2}

\displaystyle=256a^{-2}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}((\eta H)^{4}+La^{-1}(\eta H)^{3}(1+(2a\eta H)^{4\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))\,.

For
D_{t,4}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,4}\|^{2}]\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

(76)

where
\operatorname{c}_{D,4}=8a^{-1}\eta H
.
For the term
D_{t,5}
, we note that the sequence
\{(\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i})\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq s\leq t\}
is martingale-difference w.r.t the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s}^{-}
. Then, applying
Lemma

16
and using (
52
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,5}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|(\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i})\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\eta_{s}^{3}\mathbb{E}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\,.

(77)

Now, using the fact that for any
s\in\{1,\dots,t\}
the sequence
\{\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq h\leq H_{s}\}
is martingale-difference w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{+}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]=\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]\leq\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{\varepsilon}^{c})H_{s}\,.

(78)

Combining (
77
), (
78
), and using
Lemma

25
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,5}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,5}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}}\,,

(79)

where

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{D,5}^{2}=4a^{-2}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\eta^{2}H+La^{-1}\eta(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+2\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma})\,.

For the last term
D_{t,6}
, we note that again for any
s\in\{1,\dots,t\}
the sequence
\{(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq h\leq H_{s}\}
is martingale-difference w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{+}
. Thus, applying
Lemma

25
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,6}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,6}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}(1+t)^{-(\gamma+\gamma_{\eta})}\,,

(80)

where we have set

\displaystyle\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}}^{2}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma_{\varepsilon}^{c})\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,,

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{D,6}^{2}

\displaystyle=4a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}}^{2}(\eta^{3}H^{2}+La^{-1}\eta^{2}H(2a\eta H)^{2(\gamma+\gamma_{\eta})\over 1-\gamma})\,.

and used that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c})u\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{\ell=h+1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{s}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{\ell-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,\ell,c})\Gamma_{s,\ell+1:H_{s}}^{c}u\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{\ell=h+1}^{H_{s}}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(\ell-1)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(H_{s}-\ell)}\|u\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}(1-\eta_{s}a)^{2(H_{s}-1)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|u\|^{2}\,.

Bound on
R_{3}

To bound each term in
R_{3}
, we can again use Hölders inequality, that is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|\|D_{t}-D_{t}^{(s,h,c)}\|]\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|^{2}]\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t}-D_{t}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\,.

Applying Minkowski’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t}-D_{t}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,1}-D_{t,1}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,2}-D_{t,2}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,3}-D_{t,3}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,4}-D_{t,4}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,5}-D_{t,5}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,6}-D_{t,6}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\,.

Also, we set

\displaystyle R_{3,i}=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|^{2}]\,\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,i}-D_{t,i}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

Perturbations of
D_{t,1}
.

Now, we should bound each perturbation term separately. For the first term, applying
Lemma

22
, Minkowski’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,1}-D_{t,1}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\right)(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\left(\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\right)(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{t}\prod_{i=1}^{\ell-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\cdot(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{\ell}-\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{\ell})\prod_{i=\ell+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\exp{\left(-a\sum\limits_{i=1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\right)}\sum\limits_{\ell=1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\left(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{\ell}-\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{\ell}\right)(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\exp{\left(-a\sum\limits_{i=1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\right)}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\left(\Gamma_{s,H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)}\right)(\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star})\|^{2}]\,.

According to
Lemma

15
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,1}-D_{t,1}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-{a\eta H\over 1-\gamma}(1+t)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|\,.

Thus, for
R_{3,1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle R_{3,1}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\exp{\left(-{a\eta H\over 1-\gamma}(1+t)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\zeta_{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}})\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}(2\eta H+La^{-1}(1+(a\eta H)^{3\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\exp{\left(-{a\eta H\over 1-\gamma}(1+t)^{1-\gamma}\right)}(\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\zeta_{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}})\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\exp{\left(-{a\eta H\over 1-\gamma}(1+t)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\,,

(81)

where we have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{1}=8\mathrm{e}(\eta H+La^{-1}(1+(a\eta H)^{3\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))(\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\zeta_{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}})\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|\,.

Perturbations of
D_{t,2}
.

Now, we can proceed with the perturbations. For the perturbations of
D_{t,2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,2}-D_{t,2}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\leq 2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\,,

since
R_{s^{\prime}}^{c^{\prime}}-R_{s^{\prime}}^{c^{\prime},(s,h,c)}=0
for
c^{\prime}\neq c
and
s^{\prime}\neq s
. This leads to the following estimation in the final bound

\displaystyle R_{3,2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}^{5/2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\zeta_{4}(\|\varepsilon\|_{\infty}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}(1+t)^{-(3\gamma-\gamma_{\eta})/2}\,,

(82)

where we also used the Cauchy-Schwartz inequality to bound
N^{-1}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|(\|\varepsilon\|_{\infty}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|)
and defined

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{2}=8\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(\eta^{2}H^{5/2}+La^{-1}(1+(2a\eta H)^{{5\over 2}+{\gamma_{\eta}\over 2\gamma}}))\zeta_{4}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})\,.

Perturbations of
D_{t,3}
.

For the perturbations of
D_{t,3}
, we have the decomposition
D_{t,3}-D_{t,3}^{(s,h,c)}=D_{t,3}^{1}+D_{t,3}^{2}
, where we define

\displaystyle D_{t,3}^{1}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{t}(\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i})(\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s^{\prime}}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s^{\prime}})

\displaystyle D_{t,3}^{2}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{t}(\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i})\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s^{\prime}}\,.

We start with estimating
\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,3}^{1}\|^{2}]
. As
\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s^{\prime}}=\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s^{\prime}}
for
s^{\prime}\neq s
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,3}^{1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\left(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\right)(\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}\|^{2}]\,,

where we have noticed that
\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{\ell}-\prod\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{\ell}
and
\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}
are independent. Now, applying
Lemma

16
and
Lemma

17
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,3}^{1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{3,1}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\,.

where we have set

\operatorname{c}_{3,1}^{2}=32a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\,.

For the second term, applying
Lemma

18
, using the martingale-difference structure of the sequence
\{\Big(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\Big)\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s^{\prime}},1\leq s^{\prime}\leq t\}
, (
B.2
) and
Lemma

25
together with (
B.2
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,3}^{2}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\|^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s^{\prime}}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s^{\prime}}\|^{2}]\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})

\displaystyle\leq 8\mathrm{e}\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{het}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{2}H_{s^{\prime}}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})

\displaystyle+4\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{4}H_{s^{\prime}}^{4}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})

\displaystyle\leq 8\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{2}H_{s^{\prime}}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{s}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle+4\mathrm{e}\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{4}H_{s^{\prime}}^{4}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{s}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 32\mathrm{e}a^{-1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})

\displaystyle+256\mathrm{e}a^{-1}\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}(\eta H)^{3}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}(1+s)^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,,

where we used that
\gamma>1/2
for the second term. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|D_{t,3}-D_{t,3}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(\operatorname{c}_{3,2}+\operatorname{c}_{3,1}\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|)\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}^{3/2}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{c}_{3,3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}N^{-1/2}(\eta H)^{3/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}(1+s)^{-3\gamma/2}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}\,,

where
\operatorname{c}_{3,2}^{2}=32\mathrm{e}a^{-1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}
and
\operatorname{c}_{3,3}^{2}=256\mathrm{e}a^{-1}\zeta_{1}^{2}\zeta_{2}^{2}
.
Now, using this bound and applying
Lemma

25
, we get

\displaystyle R_{3,3}

\displaystyle\leq 4(\operatorname{c}_{3,2}^{1/2}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}+\operatorname{c}_{3,1}^{1/2}\zeta_{4})\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{5/2}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})

\displaystyle+\operatorname{c}_{3,3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}(\eta H)^{3/2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}(1+s)^{-3\gamma/2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{4})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{3,4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\operatorname{c}_{3,5}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-3\gamma/2}\,,

(83)

where we have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,4}

\displaystyle=2(\operatorname{c}_{3,2}^{1/2}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}+\operatorname{c}_{3,1}^{1/2}\zeta_{4})(\eta^{3/2}H+La^{-1}\eta^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{1+{3\gamma_{\eta}\over 2\gamma}})(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})\,,

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,5}

\displaystyle=2\operatorname{c}_{3,3}((\eta H)^{5/2}+La^{-1}(\eta H)^{3/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{5/2+\gamma_{\eta}/\gamma})(\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{4})\,.

Perturbations of
D_{t,5}
.

Note that for
D_{t,5}
, we have the following decomposition

\displaystyle D_{t,5}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}}\eta_{s^{\prime}}\Big(\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\Big)\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime}}\varepsilon^{c^{\prime}}(Z_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}}\eta_{s^{\prime}}\Big(\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\Big)(\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime}}-\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime},(s,h,c)})\varepsilon^{c^{\prime}}(Z_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i})\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)}(\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})-\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}^{\prime}))

\displaystyle+\sum_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}}\eta_{s^{\prime}}(\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i})\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime},(s,h,c)}\varepsilon^{c^{\prime}}(Z_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}}^{s,h,c})=T_{5,1}+T_{5,2}+T_{5,3}+T_{5,4}\,.

(84)

We can see that in the perturbation
D_{t,5}-D_{t,5}^{(s,h,c)}
there are only three terms
T_{1},T_{2}
and
T_{3}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,5}-D_{t,5}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\leq 4\mathbb{E}[\|T_{5,1}\|^{2}]+4\mathbb{E}[\|T_{5,2}\|^{2}]+4\mathbb{E}[\|T_{5,3}\|^{2}]\,.

Further, we bound these terms separately. For
T_{5,1}
, using the martingale-difference structure of the sequences
\{\Big(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\Big)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),s\in[t]\}
and
\{\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),h\in[H_{s}]\}
, applying
Lemma

18
and
Lemma

25
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[

\displaystyle\|T_{5,1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{t}\sum_{c^{\prime}=1}^{N}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H_{s^{\prime}}}\Big(\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\Big)\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime}}\varepsilon^{c^{\prime}}(Z_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{t}\sum_{c^{\prime}=1}^{N}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{H_{s^{\prime}}}\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime}}\varepsilon^{c^{\prime}}(Z_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\sum_{s^{\prime}=1}^{s-1}\eta_{s^{\prime}}^{2}H_{s^{\prime}}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{s}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{5,1}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,,

where

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{5,1}^{2}=32\mathrm{e}a^{-1}L\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma\over 1-\gamma})\,.

For the term
T_{5,2}
, we note that
\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime}}-\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime},(s,h,c)}=0
for
s^{\prime}\neq s
. Hence, applying
Lemma

16
and
Lemma

15
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|T_{5,2}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\mathbb{E}[\|(\Gamma_{s,h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h^{\prime}+1:H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h^{\prime},c})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma_{s,h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h^{\prime}+1:H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{5,2}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{5}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})\,,

where we have set
\operatorname{c}_{5,2}^{2}=32a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}
.
For the term
T_{5,3}
, applying
Lemma

16
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|T_{5,3}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{5,3}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})\,,

where we define
\operatorname{c}_{5,3}^{2}=4a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}
. Thus, combining the above bounds, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,5}-D_{t,5}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\leq\operatorname{c}_{5,4}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(1+\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}}))\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})\,,

where we have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{5,4}^{2}=4\operatorname{c}_{5,1}^{2}+4\operatorname{c}_{5,2}^{2}+4\operatorname{c}_{5,3}^{2}\,.

Thus, again applying
Lemma

25
, we get

\displaystyle R_{3,5}

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{c}_{5,4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{5/2}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}+\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}})(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{5,5}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,,

(85)

where

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{5,5}=4\operatorname{c}_{5,4}(\eta^{3/2}H+L\eta^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{2\gamma+3\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))(\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}+\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}})(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})\,.

Perturbations of
D_{t,6}
.

Firstly, we note that

\displaystyle D_{t,6}-D_{t,6}^{(s,h,c)}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}}\eta_{s^{\prime}}\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}(\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime},(s,h,c)}-\Gamma_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime}+1:H_{s^{\prime}}}^{c^{\prime}})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s^{\prime},h^{\prime},c^{\prime}})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)})(\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})-\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}^{\prime}))

\displaystyle=T_{6,1}+T_{6,2}\,.

(86)

We can see that the first term is zero for
s^{\prime}\neq s
,
c^{\prime}\neq c
and
h^{\prime}\geq h
. Thus, applying
Lemma

15
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|T_{6,1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{h^{\prime}=1}^{h-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma_{s,h^{\prime}+1:H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)}-\Gamma_{s,h^{\prime}+1:H_{s}}^{c}\|^{2}]\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle\leq 16\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})\,.

The second term
T_{6,2}
ca be bounded, as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|T_{6,2}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathbb{E}[\|G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\|^{2}]\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})\,.

Combining together the last two inequalities, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t,6}-D_{t,6}^{(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\leq 34\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\varepsilon})\,.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle R_{3,6}

\displaystyle\leq 17\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}^{3/2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{6}(1+t)^{-(\gamma+\gamma_{\eta})/2}\,,

(87)

where we have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{6}=68\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{noise}}(\eta^{2}H^{3/2}+La^{-1}\eta H^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{3(\gamma+\gamma_{\eta})\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\,.

Finalized Berry-Essen bound

We return to the decomposition (
69
). We get

\displaystyle R_{1}\leq\operatorname{c}_{R,1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{3}N^{-1/2}\eta^{1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,.

where
\operatorname{c}_{R,1}=259d^{1/2}8a^{-1}\eta^{1/2}
. For
R_{2}
, we apply (
72
) and combine together (
73
), (
74
), (
75
), (
76
), (

), (
80
), thus we obtain

\displaystyle R_{2}\leq\operatorname{c}_{D,1}d^{1/2}N^{1/2}\exp{\left(-a\eta Ht/2\right)}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|

\displaystyle+(\operatorname{c}_{D,2}+\operatorname{c}_{D,3,2}+\operatorname{c}_{D,4})d^{1/2}N^{1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}

\displaystyle+(\operatorname{c}_{D,3,1}+\operatorname{c}_{D,5}+\operatorname{c}_{D,6})d^{1/2}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,.

For
R_{3}
, we use the bounds (

), (

), (

), (

), (
