), and get

\displaystyle R_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{1}\exp{\left(-{a\eta H\over 1-\gamma}(1+t)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|+\operatorname{c}_{2}(1+t)^{-(3\gamma-\gamma_{\eta})/2}

\displaystyle+\operatorname{c}_{3,5}(1+t)^{-3\gamma/2}+(\operatorname{c}_{3,4}+\operatorname{c}_{5,5}+\operatorname{c}_{6})N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,,

where
\gamma=\gamma_{\eta}-\gamma_{H}
.

B.3
Lower bound for Gaussian approximation

To illustrate the inherent limitations of Gaussian approximation,
we consider a simple
1
-dimensional linear stochastic approximation with a single agent
and constant local updates (
H_{t}=1
for all
t\geq 1
):

\bar{\mathbf{A}}\theta_{\star}=\bar{\mathbf{b}},\qquad\bar{\mathbf{A}}=1,\quad\bar{\mathbf{b}}=0.

Comparing with the general setting, here we have
\gamma_{H}=0
and
\gamma_{\eta}=\gamma\in(0,1)
. The unbiased stochastic estimates are given by

\mathbf{A}(Z_{j})=1+\xi_{j},\qquad\mathbf{b}(Z_{j})=\bar{\mathbf{b}},

where
\xi_{j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
. Then,
FedLSA
reduces to

\theta_{t}=\theta_{t-1}-\eta_{t}(\mathbf{A}(Z_{t})\theta_{t-1}-\mathbf{b}(Z_{t})),\quad\theta_{0}=0.

(88)

Unrolling the recursion gives

\theta_{t}=-\sum_{j=1}^{t}\eta_{j}\prod_{\ell=j+1}^{t}(1-\eta_{\ell})\xi_{j},

and its variance under step-size scaling satisfies

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\theta_{t}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,v_{t}),\quad v_{t}:=\mathrm{Var}[\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\theta_{t}]=\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{t}\eta_{j}^{2}\prod_{\ell=j+1}^{t}(1-\eta_{\ell})^{2}.

(89)

Under this setting, we can state the following results

Proposition 3
.

Assume that
\eta_{1}=0
and
\eta_{\ell}=(1+\ell)^{-\gamma}
with
0<\gamma<1
. For
1
-dimensional single agent
FedLSA
with
\bar{\mathbf{A}}\theta_{\star}=\bar{\mathbf{b}},\bar{\mathbf{A}}=1,\bar{\mathbf{b}}=0
and
\mathbf{A}(Z_{j})=1+\xi_{j},\mathbf{b}(Z_{j})=\bar{\mathbf{b}}
with
\xi_{j}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{t\to\infty}v_{t}:=\lim_{t\to\infty}\mathrm{Var}[\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\theta_{t}]=\frac{1}{2}\,,

where
v_{t}
is defined in (
89
). Moreover, for any
t\geq 4
, it can be shown that

\displaystyle\Big|v_{t}-\frac{1}{2}\Big|\geq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v}}(t^{\gamma-1}+t^{-\gamma})\,,

for some positive constant
\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v}}>0
that depends only on
\gamma
.

From
Proposition

3
it follows that the limiting variance of
v_{t}
is
\sigma_{\infty}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}
. Moreover,
Proposition

3
gives us the lower bound for
|v_{t}-\sigma_{\infty}^{2}|=|v_{t}-\frac{1}{2}|\geq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v}}(t^{\gamma-1}+t^{-\gamma})
. Consequently, by
11
, the convex distance between
\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star})
and limiting distribution
\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{\infty}^{2})
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}\big(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}),\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma_{\infty}^{2})\big)\gtrsim t^{\gamma-1}+t^{-\gamma},

and the statement follows.

Proof of
Proposition

3
.

We begin by noting that

\displaystyle\eta_{j}^{2}={1\over 2}\eta_{j}(1-(1-\eta_{j})^{2})+{1\over 2}\eta_{j}^{3}\,.

Denoting
P_{j,t}:=\prod_{\ell=j+1}^{t}(1-\eta_{\ell})^{2}
, we can write

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{-1}v_{t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t}\eta_{j}(P_{j,t}-P_{j-1,t})+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t}\eta_{j}^{3}P_{j,t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}S_{t,1}+\frac{1}{2}S_{t,2}\,.

We first consider the term
S_{t,1}
. By applying Abel’s summation by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t}\eta_{j}(P_{j,t}-P_{j-1,t})

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}P_{t,t}-\eta_{1}P_{0,t}+\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(\eta_{j}-\eta_{j+1})P_{j,t}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}+\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(\eta_{j}-\eta_{j+1})P_{j,t}-\eta_{1}P_{0,t}\,.

Using the inequalities
(j+1)^{-\gamma}-(j+2)^{-\gamma}\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}(j+1)^{-\gamma-1}
and
\log(1-x)\geq-4x
for
x\in[0,1/2]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(\eta_{j}-\eta_{j+1})P_{j,t}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(j+1)^{-\gamma-1}\exp\Big(-4\sum_{\ell=j+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(j+1)^{-\gamma-1}\exp\Big(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\big(t^{1-\gamma}-(j+1)^{1-\gamma}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{2}\exp\Big(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\Big)\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(j+1)^{-\gamma-1}\exp\Big(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}(j+1)^{1-\gamma}\Big)\,.

Since the function
f(x)=(x+1)^{-\gamma-1}\exp\big(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}(x+1)^{1-\gamma}\big)
is strictly increasing for
x\geq 1
, we can bound the sum by the corresponding integral:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{t-1}(\eta_{j}-\eta_{j+1})P_{j,t}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\int_{1}^{t-1}(x+1)^{-\gamma-1}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}(x+1)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{2}t^{-\gamma}\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\int_{2/t}^{1}u^{-(1+\gamma)}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}u^{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}t^{-\gamma}\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\int_{1/2}^{1}u^{-(1+\gamma)}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}u^{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}u\,.

For
t\geq 4
we may estimate asymptotic behaviour of the integral using Laplace approximation introduced in, for instance,
15
and
39
:

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma}{2}t^{-\gamma}\int_{1/2}^{1}u^{-(1+\gamma)}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}u^{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}u=\frac{\gamma}{8}t^{-1}\left(1+\mathcal{O}(t^{\gamma-1})\right)\,.

Therefore, as
\eta_{1}P_{0,t}\leq\prod_{\ell=1}^{t}(1-\eta_{\ell})^{2}
is exponentially small, we may write

\displaystyle\begin{cases}S_{t,1}=\eta_{t}+R_{t,1}\,,\\
R_{t,1}\geq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v,1}}t^{-1}\end{cases}

for some positive constant
\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v,1}}
depending only on
\gamma
.

Next, we consider
S_{t,2}
. Following a similar approach as for
S_{t,1}
and approximating the sum with an integral, we have

\displaystyle S_{t,2}

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{t}\eta_{j}^{3}P_{j,t}\geq\sum\limits_{j=1}^{t}(1+j)^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(-4\sum\limits_{\ell=j+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\geq\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{t}(j+1)^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}(j+1)^{1-\gamma}\right)}

\displaystyle\geq\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}\int_{1}^{t}(x+1)^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}(x+1)^{1-\gamma}\right)}

\displaystyle=\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}(t+1)^{1-3\gamma}\int_{2/(t+1)}^{1}u^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}u^{1-\gamma}(t+1)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\geq\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}(t+1)^{1-3\gamma}\int_{1/2}^{1}u^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}u^{1-\gamma}(t+1)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}u\,.

Here we also use the fact that function
f(x)=(x+1)^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}\right)}
is strictly increasing on
[1,\infty)
. Now we determine the asymptotic behaviour of the following integral using Laplace approximation

\displaystyle\exp{\left(-\frac{4}{1-\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}\right)}(t+1)^{1-3\gamma}\int_{1/2}^{1}u^{-3\gamma}\exp{\left(\frac{4}{1-\gamma}u^{1-\gamma}(t+1)^{1-\gamma}\right)}\mathrm{d}u=\frac{1}{4}t^{-2\gamma}(1+\mathcal{O}(t^{\gamma-1}))\,.

Finally, taking
\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v}}:=\min(\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v,1}},\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v,2}})
yields the desired lower bound

\displaystyle\Big|v_{t}-\frac{1}{2}\Big|\geq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf v}}\big(t^{\gamma-1}+t^{-\gamma}\big)\,.

∎

Appendix C
Multiplier bootstrap validity

We start with the expression for
t_{0}
in
4
.

A’ 4
.

We assume that

\displaystyle t\geq t_{0}=\max\left(N,\mathrm{e}^{3},\left({4C_{\infty,1}\over 2\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathbf{A}})\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})}\right)^{1/(1-\gamma)},\left({4C_{\infty,2}\over 2\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathbf{A}})\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})}\right)^{1/\gamma},n_{0}\right)

where
n_{0}
satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\left({2\operatorname{c}_{U}\eta\over N^{3}}\right)^{1/2}(1+n_{0})^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\sqrt{\log{(2dn_{0})}}+{U_{\max}\eta\over 3N^{2}}(1+n_{0})^{-\gamma_{\eta}}\log{(2dn_{0})}&\leq N^{-1}\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg})/2\,,\\
\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{M,2}^{\mathsf{b}}N^{-2}\log^{6}(n_{0})(1+n_{0})^{-\gamma_{\eta}}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{M,3}^{\mathsf{b}}N^{-1}\log^{3}(n_{0})(1+n_{0})^{-2\gamma}&\leq N^{-1}\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg})/2\,,\end{cases}

for constants
\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{M,2}^{\mathsf{b}},\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{M,3}^{\mathsf{b}}
defined in
Lemma

14
and
\operatorname{c}_{U},U_{\max}
from
Lemma

12
.

We set the events under which we will consider the normal approximation in bootstrap world

\displaystyle\Omega_{1}

\displaystyle=\{Z\in\mathcal{Z}:\forall c\in[N]\,,\|(\mathrm{I}-\eta\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z))u\|\leq\mathrm{e}(1-\eta a)\|u\|\}\,,

\displaystyle\Omega_{2}

\displaystyle=\{(\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}])^{1/p}\leq\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1}(1+t)^{1/p-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{1/p-\gamma}\}\,,

\displaystyle\Omega_{3}

\displaystyle=\{\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\lesssim_{\log_{t}}N^{-3/2}t^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+N^{-1}t^{-2\gamma}\}

\displaystyle\Omega_{0}

\displaystyle=\Omega_{1}\cap\Omega_{2}\cap\Omega_{3}\,.

(90)

In addition, throughout this section we use the notation

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}=\sigma(w_{t,k,c},Z_{t,k,c}\mid t>s\text{ or }t=s\text{ and }k\geq h)\,,\quad\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},-}=\sigma(w_{t,k,c},Z_{t,k,c}\mid t<s\text{ or }t=s\text{ and }k\leq h)\,.

We start with the definition of the bootstrap procedure. We consider a set of random variables
\mathcal{W}^{t}=\{w_{s,h,c},s\in[t],h\in[H_{s}],c\in[N]\}
, where
w_{s,h,c}
are i.i.d and independent of
\mathcal{Z}^{t}=\{Z_{s,h,c},s\in[t],h\in[H_{s}],c\in[N]\}
, with
\mathbb{E}[w_{1,1,1}]=1
,
\mathrm{Var}[w_{1,1,1}]=1
, and for any
p>2
, we set
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[|w_{s,h,c}-1|^{p}]=m_{p}<\infty
. Then, we denote
\mathbb{P}^{\mathsf{b}}=\mathbb{P}(\cdot|\mathcal{Z}^{t})
and
\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}=\mathbb{E}(\cdot|\mathcal{Z}^{t})
. According to multiplier bootstrap procedure, we slightly modify the iterations (
6
) introducing multiplicative noise, that is,

\displaystyle\theta_{t,h}^{\mathsf{b},c}=\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\eta_{t}w_{t,h,c}(\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\mathbf{b}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c}))\,.

(91)

C.1
Proof of the Theorem
4

Proof.

The goal of the
Theorem

4
is to estimate the quantity

\displaystyle\sup_{B\in\text{Conv}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\left|\mathbb{P}^{\mathsf{b}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t})\in B)-\mathbb{P}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star})\in B)\right|\,.

From now, we restrict ourselves to the event
\Omega_{0}
. Restricting to this
event, we obtain that, with triangle inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{B\in\text{Conv}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{P}^{\mathsf{b}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t})\in B)-\mathbb{P}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star})\in B)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{b}}\big(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t}),Y^{\mathsf{b}}\big)+\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}\big(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}-\theta_{\star}),Y\big)+\sup_{B\in\text{Conv}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\left|\mathbb{P}(Y\in B)-\mathbb{P}^{\mathsf{b}}(Y^{\mathsf{b}}\in B)\right|\,.

Now we control the first term using
Theorem

5
, second one using
Theorem

3
and the third one using
Lemma

21
together with

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}-\mathrm{I}\|\leq\|\Sigma_{t}^{-1}\|\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\lesssim_{\log_{t}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}^{2}(N^{-1/2}t^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+t^{-2\gamma})\,.

We show that
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{0})\geq 1-\frac{4}{t}
in
Lemma

9
.
∎

Lemma 9
.

Let
\Omega_{0}=\Omega_{1}\cap\Omega_{2}\cap\Omega_{3}
be the event defined in (
90
). Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{0})\geq 1-\frac{4}{t},

where
t\geq 1
denotes the number of global iterations of
FedLSA
.

Proof.

We first bound the probability of
\Omega_{2}^{c}
. By Markov’s inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{2}^{c})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]}{\left(\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{1/p-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(1+t)^{1/p-\gamma}\right)^{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]}{t\left(\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\right)^{p}}\leq\frac{1}{t}.

Thus,
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{2})\geq 1-\frac{1}{t}
.

Next, applying
Lemma

10
with
p=\log(Nt)
and using a union bound over
c\in[N]
, we obtain that on the event
\Omega_{1}
,

\displaystyle\|(\mathrm{I}-\eta\mathbf{A}^{c}(z))u\|\leq\mathrm{e}(1-\eta a)\|u\|,

and
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{1})\geq 1-\frac{1}{t}
.

Further, by
Proposition

6
, we have

\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{3})\geq 1-\frac{2}{t}.

Finally, applying the union bound to the events
\Omega_{1}
,
\Omega_{2}
, and
\Omega_{3}
yields

\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{0})=\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{1}\cap\Omega_{2}\cap\Omega_{3})\geq 1-\frac{4}{t},

which concludes the proof.
∎

C.2
Rate of Gaussian approximation in the bootstrap world

Now, our aim is to quantify the convergence rate of the convex distance in the bootstrap world

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{b}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t}),Y^{\mathsf{b}})=\sup_{B\in\mathsf{C}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}\left|\mathbb{P}^{\mathsf{b}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t})\in B)-\mathbb{P}^{\mathsf{b}}(Y^{\mathsf{b}}\in B)\right|\,,

where
Y^{\mathsf{b}}\sim\mathrm{N}(0,\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})
and
\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}
is defined in (
92
). We start with the decomposition

\displaystyle\theta_{t,h}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,h}^{c}

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c}))(\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,h-1}^{c})

\displaystyle+\eta_{t}(\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c},Z_{t,h,c})+\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\bar{\mathbf{b}}^{c})-\eta_{t}w_{t,h,c}(\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c},Z_{t,h,c})+\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\bar{\mathbf{b}}^{c})

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c}))(\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,h-1}^{c})-\eta_{t}(w_{t,h,c}-1)(\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})+\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})(\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta^{c}_{\star}))

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}w_{t,h,c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c}))(\theta_{t,h-1}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,h-1}^{c})-\eta_{t}(w_{t,h,c}-1)\tilde{\varepsilon}^{c}_{t,h}\,.

Here, we define

\tilde{\varepsilon}^{c}_{t,h}=\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})+\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})(\theta_{t,h-1}^{c}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\,.

We also define the bootstrap counterparts of random matrices product

\displaystyle\Gamma_{t,\ell:r}^{\mathsf{b},c}=\prod_{h=l}^{r}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}w_{t,h,c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,h,c}))\,,\quad\Gamma_{t}^{\mathsf{b},c}=\Gamma_{t,1:H_{t}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\,,\quad\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{t}=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\Gamma_{t}^{\mathsf{b},c}\,.

Setting
h=H_{t}
and unrolling the recursion, we get

\displaystyle\theta_{t,H_{t}}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,H_{t}}^{c}=\Gamma_{t,H_{t}}^{\mathsf{b},c}(\theta_{t,0}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,0}^{c})-\eta_{t}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{t}}(w_{t,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{t,h+1:H_{t}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{c}_{t,h}\,,

Averaging over clients, we obtain

\displaystyle\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t}

\displaystyle=N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\{\theta_{t,H_{t}}^{\mathsf{b},c}-\theta_{t,H_{t}}^{c}\}

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{t}(\theta_{t-1}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t-1})-\eta_{t}\underbrace{N^{-1}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{t}}(w_{t,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{t,h+1:H_{t}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\tilde{\varepsilon}^{c}_{t,h}}_{\mathcal{E}^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{t}}

\displaystyle=\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}(\theta_{0}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{0})-\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\mathcal{E}^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{s}\,.

Again, in this decomposition we isolate the leading term with
\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{t,h,c})
, that is,

\displaystyle\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{-N^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})}_{\text{Leading term}}+\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}(\theta_{0}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{0})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i})\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})

\displaystyle-N^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{s,h-1}^{c}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\,.

The second term here vanish since
\theta_{0}^{\mathsf{b}}=\theta_{0}
. Further, we can extract the heterogeneous bias from the last term, and get the completed leading term

\displaystyle M_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})

\displaystyle-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})\,,

\displaystyle\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}[M_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}(M_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{T}]\,.

(92)

Analogously to the case of
M_{t}
, we can further specify the leading term of
M_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}
, as,

\displaystyle M_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\left(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\right)\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})

\displaystyle+N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c})\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})

\displaystyle=M_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}+M_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}+M_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}\,.

Therefore, we can state our final decomposition of self-normalized statistic in the boostrap wolrd
\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t})
in the following form

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t})=W_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\,,

(93)

where we have set

\displaystyle W_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}M_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\upsilon_{s,h,c}^{\mathsf{b}}\,,

\displaystyle\upsilon_{s,h,c}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=-N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})\,,

and
D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}=D_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t,4}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t,5}^{\mathsf{b}}
, with

\displaystyle D_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}N^{-1}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i})\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}N^{-1}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=-\eta_{t}^{-1/2}N^{-1}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{s,h-1}^{c}-\theta_{\star})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,4}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}N^{-1}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i})\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\,,

\displaystyle D_{t,5}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}^{-1/2}N^{-1}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\,.

Using the decomposition (
93
) together with

47
, we are now ready to state the main result of this section.

Theorem 5
.

(Gaussian approximation in the "boostrap world")

Let
Y^{\mathsf{b}}\sim\mathrm{N}(0,\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})
and
\eta H<\beta_{\infty}
. Under the assumptions
1
(
\log(Nt)
),
2
,
2
,
3
and
4
, on the event
\Omega_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{b}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t}),Y^{\mathsf{b}})\leq M_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}N^{-1/2}\log^{6}(t)(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+M_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}\log^{4}(t)(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

(94)

where the event
\Omega_{0}
i defined in (
90
) and
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{0})\geq 1-{4\over t}
.

Proof.

We start by applying
Proposition

5
to the statistics
X=W_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}
and
Y=D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}
, that is,

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{b}}(\eta_{t}^{-1/2}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}(\theta_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\theta_{t}),Z^{\mathsf{b}})\leq\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{b}}(W_{t}^{\mathsf{b}},Z^{\mathsf{b}})+2c_{d}^{p/(p+1)}(\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}])^{1/(1+p)}\,.

(95)

where
Z^{\mathsf{b}}\sim\mathrm{N}(0,\mathrm{I})
. Therefore, applying
47
, we estimate

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{C}}^{\mathsf{b}}(W_{t}^{\mathsf{b}},Y^{\mathsf{b}})\leq 259d^{1/2}\Upsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\,,

where
\Upsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum\limits_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}[\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|^{3}]
. We note that for any
s\in[t],h\in[H_{s}]
and
c\in[N]
, using
Proposition

6
, we have

\displaystyle\|\upsilon_{s,h,c}\|\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\eta_{s}|w_{s,h,c}-1|\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,.

Thus, applying
Lemma

25
, on the event
\Omega_{1}
, we get

\displaystyle\Upsilon_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}^{3}N^{-3/2}\eta_{t}^{-3/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}[|w_{s,h,c}-1|^{3}]\|\prod_{i=1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{3}\|\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\|^{3}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq W_{\max}^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}^{3}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-3/2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}\exp{\left(-3a\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}H_{i}\right)}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq 2W_{\max}^{3}(\eta^{2}H+La^{-1}\eta(1+(3a\eta H)^{2\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma\over 1-\gamma}))\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}^{3}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{3}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{\Upsilon}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}^{3}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{3}\,.

Then, on the event
\Omega_{2}
with
p=\log(t)-1
, applying
Proposition

4
, we bound the second term of (
95
), as

\displaystyle(\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}])^{1/(p+1)}

\displaystyle\leq(\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{1/p-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(1+t)^{1/p-\gamma})^{p/(p+1)}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}})^{p/(p+1)}p^{6}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{1/(p+1)-\gamma_{\eta}/2}(1+t)^{\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(p+1)}

\displaystyle+(1+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}})^{p/(p+1)}p^{4}(1+t)^{1/(p+1)-\gamma+\gamma/(p+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}^{2+\gamma_{\eta}/2}(1+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}})N^{-1/2}\log^{6}(t)(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}

\displaystyle+\mathrm{e}^{3/2+\gamma}(1+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}})\log^{4}(t)(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,.

Setting the constants

\displaystyle M_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{\Upsilon}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}^{3}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{3}+\mathrm{e}^{2+\gamma_{\eta}/2}(1+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}})\,,

\displaystyle M_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{e}^{3/2+\gamma}(1+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}})\,,

we obtain the result (
94
).
∎

In the following proposition, we assume that the expectation is taken over the probability space generated by
\mathcal{W}^{t}
and
\mathcal{Z}^{t}
.

Proposition 4
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
(
p
),

2
,

3
, and
4
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

where the constants
\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}
,
\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}
are defined in (
96
).

Proof.

Firstly, we apply the Minkowski’s inequality, that is,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t,4}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]+\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|D_{t,5}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\,.

To bound
D_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}
, using that the sequence
\{\left(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\right)(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\}
is martingale-difference w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
and applying Burkholder’s inequality
[
40
, Theorem 8.6]
, together with
Lemma

25
and
Lemma

20
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq W_{\max}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{b}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}^{1/2}a^{-1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{b}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,,

where, again applying Burkholder’s inqequality, we used the bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq W_{\max}p^{2}N^{1/2}H_{s}^{1/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,,

and have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}=2\mathrm{e}^{1/2}a^{-1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(\eta^{1/2}H^{1/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(1+(a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,\,.

Further, using the martingale-difference structure of the sequence
\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq s\leq t\}
w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
, applying Burkholder’s inequality and
Lemma

25
, we bound
D_{t,2}
, as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}(1+t)^{-(\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma)/2}\,,

where, applying Burkholder’s inequality and the fact that the sequence
\{(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq h\leq H_{s}\}
is martingale-difference w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
, we used the bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}

\displaystyle(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{3}N^{1/2}\eta_{s}H_{s}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}

\displaystyle(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[|w_{s,h,c}-1|^{p}]\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{2}\eta_{s}H_{s}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,.

Here, we also have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}=4\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(\eta H+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(\eta H)^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,\,.

Now, in order to bound
D_{t,4}^{\mathsf{b}}
, we note that the sequence
\{\left(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\right)(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq h\leq H_{s}\}
is martingale-difference w.r.t the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
. Then, applying Burkholder’s inequality together with
Lemma

25
and
Lemma

20
, defining

\displaystyle S_{s,4}^{\mathsf{b}}=\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{p}]\,,

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,4}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq p\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]S_{s,4}^{\mathsf{b}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}W_{\max}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq e^{1/2}a^{-1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\zeta_{3}W_{\max}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{4}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,,

where we have used that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}

\displaystyle(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3}W_{\max}p^{2}N^{1/2}H_{s}^{1/2}\,,

and have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{4}^{\mathsf{b}}=2\mathrm{e}^{1/2}a^{-1/2}\zeta_{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(\eta^{1/2}H^{1/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(1+(a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\,\,.

Finally, to bound
D_{t,5}
, we again use the martingale-difference structure of the underlying sequence
\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c}),1\leq s\leq t\}
w.r.t the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
and apply Burkholder’s inequality together with
Lemma

25
, thus, denoting

\displaystyle S_{s,5}^{\mathsf{b}}=\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{p}]\,,

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,5}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq p\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]S_{s,5}^{\mathsf{b}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\zeta_{3}W_{\max}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{5}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}(1+t)^{-(\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma)/2}\,,

where we have used the bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}

\displaystyle(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c})\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq W_{\max}\zeta_{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}p^{3}N^{1/2}\eta_{s}H_{s}\,,

and have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{5}^{\mathsf{b}}=4W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\zeta_{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}(\eta H+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(\eta H)^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))\,\,.

For the term
D_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}
, we first note that

\displaystyle\theta_{s,h-1}^{c}-\theta_{\star}=\Gamma_{s,h-1}^{c}(\theta_{s-1}-\theta_{\star})-\eta_{s}\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}\Gamma_{s,j+1:h-1}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})+(\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,h-1}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\,.

This decomposition separates
D_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}
into three terms, that is,
D_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}=D_{t,3,1}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t,3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}+D_{t,3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}
. For the term
D_{t,3,1}
, using the martingale-difference structure of the sequence
\{\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\Gamma_{s,h-1}^{c}(\theta_{s-1}-\theta_{\star})\}
w.r.t the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
and applying Burkholder’s inequality together with
Corollary

5
, setting

\displaystyle S_{s,3,1}^{\mathsf{b}}=\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\Gamma_{s,h-1}^{c}(\theta_{s-1}-\theta_{\star})\|^{p}]\,,

we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,3,1}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}S_{s,3,1}^{\mathsf{b}}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\theta_{s-1}-\theta_{\star}\|^{p}]\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+4\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{6}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}(1+s)^{-\gamma_{\eta}}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+4\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}(1+s)^{-2\gamma}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{3,1,2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+(\operatorname{c}_{3,1,1}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{t,3,1,3}^{\mathsf{b}})p^{3}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,.

where we have used that
x^{1/2}e^{-cx}\leq(c\mathrm{e})^{-1/2}e^{-cx/2}
together with the bound

\displaystyle\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\exp{\left(-{a\over 2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\leq e^{a/(1-\gamma)}\left({2(\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma)\over a\mathrm{e}\eta H}\right)^{\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma\over 1-\gamma}\eta^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

and have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,1,1}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=4a^{-1/2}e^{1/2+a/(1-\gamma)}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\,W_{\max}\left({2(\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma)\over a\mathrm{e}}\right)^{\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma\over 1-\gamma}\eta^{-1/2}\|\theta_{0}-\theta_{\star}\|\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\,,

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,1,2}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=8\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}((\eta H)^{1/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+2\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\,\,,

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,1,3}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=8\operatorname{c}_{\text{last},2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}((\eta H)^{1/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{3\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\,\,.

Similarly, for the term
D_{t,3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}
, applying Burkholder’s inequality together with
Lemma

25
, setting

\displaystyle S_{s,3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}=\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}\Gamma_{s,j+1:h-1}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\,,

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]S_{s,3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{4}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(1+t)^{-(\gamma_{\eta}+\gamma)/2}\,,

where we have used that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}\Gamma_{s,j+1:h-1}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\leq p\left(\sum_{j=1}^{h-1}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\Gamma_{s,j+1:h-1}^{c}\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}\leq pH_{s}^{1/2}\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,,

and have set

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}=4W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(\eta H)^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}\,\,.

Finally, to bound
D_{t,3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}
, we again apply Burkholder’s inequality and
Lemma

25
, thus, setting

\displaystyle S_{s,3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}=\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,h-1}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\|^{p}]\,,

we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|D_{t,3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]S_{s,3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq W_{\max}\zeta_{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

where we defined

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}=8W_{\max}\zeta_{3}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}((\eta H)^{3/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}\eta H(1+(2a\eta H)^{3\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))\,\,.

Finally, setting

\displaystyle\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{1}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{3,1,3}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{4}^{\mathsf{b}}

(96)

\displaystyle\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{3,1,1}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{2}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{3,1,3}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{3,2}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{3,3}^{\mathsf{b}}+\operatorname{c}_{5}^{\mathsf{b}}\,,

we complete the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Experimental Results

In this section, we provide the experimental results for the normal approximation of FedLSA. Code is available in
https://github.com/levensons/FedLSA_NormApprox
. We consider the Garnet environment
1
,
17
with
n=30
states, dimension
d=5
, number of actions
a=2
, and branching number
b=2
. We then generate
N=5
heterogeneous Garnet environments with small perturbations. The process for generating heterogeneous environments follows that described in
34
. In our experiment, we first sample a unit vector
u\in\mathbb{S}_{d-1}
onto which we project the obtained confidence sets. Then, we sample
R=1024
trajectories
\{Z_{s,h,c}^{r},s\in[t],h\in[H_{s}],c\in[N]\}_{r=1}^{1024}
and, for each of them, additionally sample
N_{\mathsf{b}}=256
bootstrap trajectories. The bootstrap weights
\{w_{s,h,c},s\in[t],h\in[H_{s}],c\in[N]\}
are i.i.d. and defined as

\displaystyle w_{s,h,c}={\tilde{w}_{s,h,c}-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{w}_{s,h,c}]\over\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}[\tilde{w}_{s,h,c}]}}\,,\quad\tilde{w}_{s,h,c}\sim\text{Beta}(0.5,2)\,.

We therefore consider several algorithms for constructing confidence sets for the last iterate
\theta_{t}
. Then, based on
R
samples, we estimate the probability that
\theta_{\star}
belongs to the confidence sets and report it as coverage.

Plug-in Estimate (PE).

As a baseline, we consider the plug-in estimate in a way similar to
28
. First, we approximate the noise covariance and design matrix at step
t
as

\displaystyle\widehat{\Sigma}_{*,t}^{\sf avg}=N^{-1}\bar{H}_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{s},Z_{s,h,c})(\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{s},Z_{s,h,c}))^{T}\,,\quad\bar{\mathbf{A}}_{t}=N^{-1}\bar{H}_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\,,

where
\bar{H}_{t}=\sum_{s=1}^{H_{t}}H_{s}
. Afterwards, we solve the Poisson equation with
\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}
replaced by
\widehat{\Sigma}_{*,t}^{\sf avg}
; that is, we find
\widehat{\Sigma}_{\infty,t}
such that

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{A}}_{t}\widehat{\Sigma}_{\infty}+\widehat{\Sigma}_{\infty}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{T}_{t}=N^{-1}\widehat{\Sigma}_{*,t}^{\sf avg}\,.

Finally, we consider the confidence interval
u^{T}\theta_{t}\pm z_{\alpha/2}\eta_{t}^{1/2}\sqrt{u^{T}\hat{\Sigma}_{\infty,t}u}
, where
z_{\alpha/2}
is the
\alpha/2
-quantile of
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
.

Empirical Quantiles (EQ).

As described in
Section

6
, we construct the confidence interval using the empirical quantiles of the bootstrap samples.

Standard Deviation-Based Confidence Intervals (SDB).

We estimate the standard deviation
\hat{\sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}(u)
of the bootstrap samples
\{\theta_{t}^{b},b\in[N_{\mathsf{b}}]\}
and consider the confidence interval
u^{T}\theta_{t}\pm z_{\alpha/2}\eta_{t}^{1/2}\hat{\sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}(u)
.

Discussion.

In
Table

3
and
Table

4
, we compare coverage across different confidence levels and iteration lengths. In our experiments, we consider a decreasing step size with
\gamma_{\eta}=0.6
. We also consider two regimes: one with a constant number of local updates,
H=20
, and another in which the number of local updates increases at the rate
\gamma_{H}=0.2
with
H=5
. In the case
\gamma_{H}=0
, we can see that the bootstrap-based methods outperform the baseline even for a small number of iterations and, for
T=6000
, produce decent coverage. On the other hand, when we consider an increasing number of local updates, for large
T
it becomes comparable to, or slightly better than, the bootstrap-based methods. This can be explained by the fact that, as the number of samples increases, the PE estimate becomes sufficiently accurate to provide good coverage.

Appendix E
Technical results

In this section we collect several auxiliary results that will be used in the proofs of the main theorems.

Proposition 5
.

Let
\nu
be a standard Gaussian measure in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Then for any random vectors
X,Y
taking values in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and any
p\geq 1
,

\sup_{B\in\mathsf{C}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}|\mathbb{P}(X+Y\in B)-\nu(B)|\leq\sup_{B\in\mathsf{C}(\mathbb{R}^{d})}|\mathbb{P}(X\in B)-\nu(B)|+2c_{d}^{p/(p+1)}\mathbb{E}^{1/(p+1)}[\|Y\|^{p}]\,,

(97)

where
c_{d}
is the isoperimetric constant of class
\mathsf{C}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Proof.

Proof and definition for the isoperimetric constant
c_{d}
can be found in
48
.
∎

Lemma 10
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1/\mathrm{e}^{2})
and let
Y
be a positive random variable, such that
\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[Y^{p}]\leq C_{1}+C_{2}p
for any
2\leq p\leq\log(1/\delta)
. Then it holds with probability at least
1-\delta
, that

\displaystyle Y\leq\mathrm{e}C_{1}+\mathrm{e}C_{2}\log(1/\delta)\,.

Proof.

For the complete proof, see
44
.
∎

Lemma 11
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
(2),

2
and

3
, choosing
\eta H\leq\beta_{\infty}
for some
\beta_{\infty}>0
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{\infty}\|\leq C_{\infty,1}(1+t)^{\gamma-1}+C_{\infty,2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

where the matrix
\Sigma_{\infty}
is the solution of the Lyapunov’s equation

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{A}}\Sigma_{\infty}+\Sigma_{\infty}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{T}=N^{-1}\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}\,,

(98)

where
\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}
is defined in (
63
).

Proof.

Due to
43
, the solution of Lyapunov’s equation (
98
) exists since the matrix
\bar{\mathbf{A}}
is Hurwitz. Let us consider only the leading part of the term
M_{t}
, that is,
\hat{M}_{t}=M_{t,1}+M_{t,2}
. Using the notation
P_{s}^{t}=\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}
, we define

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\hat{M}_{t}\hat{M}_{t}^{T}]=N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}P_{s}^{t}\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}(P_{s}^{t})^{T}

To estimate the difference between
\Sigma_{t}
and
\Sigma_{\infty}
, we write the recursion

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}\hat{\Sigma}_{t}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1})^{T}+N^{-1}\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}

\displaystyle=\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}\{\hat{\Sigma}_{t}+\hat{\Sigma}_{t}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})^{T}+(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\}

\displaystyle+\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})\hat{\Sigma}_{t}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})^{T}+N^{-1}\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}

\displaystyle=\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}\{\bar{\mathbf{A}}\hat{\Sigma}_{t}+\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{T}-N^{-1}\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}\}

\displaystyle+(\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-1)(\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\bar{R}_{t+1}^{T}-\bar{R}_{t+1}\hat{\Sigma}_{t})

\displaystyle+\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})\hat{\Sigma}_{t}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})^{T}\,,

where we have used the identity

\displaystyle(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t+1}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{H_{t+1}}-\mathrm{I}

\displaystyle=-\eta_{t+1}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}\sum\limits_{h=1}^{H_{t+1}}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t+1}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}

\displaystyle=-\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}-\underbrace{\eta_{t+1}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{t+1}}\{(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t+1}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{c})^{h-1}-\mathrm{I}\}}_{R_{t+1}^{c}}\,.

Now, the error
E_{t}=\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\Sigma_{\infty}
evolves as

\displaystyle E_{t+1}

\displaystyle=E_{t}-\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}\{\bar{\mathbf{A}}E_{t}+E_{t}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{T}\}

\displaystyle+(\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}-1)(\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\bar{R}_{t+1}^{T}-\bar{R}_{t+1}\hat{\Sigma}_{t})+\eta_{t}\eta_{t+1}^{-1}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})\hat{\Sigma}_{t}(G^{\sf avg}_{t+1}-\mathrm{I})^{T}

\displaystyle=E_{t}-\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}\{\bar{\mathbf{A}}E_{t}+E_{t}\bar{\mathbf{A}}^{T}\}+r_{t+1}\,.

To quantify the convergence, we transition the recursion into the vectorized form. Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\text{vec}(E_{t+1})=(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1}((\mathrm{I}\otimes\bar{\mathbf{A}})+(\bar{\mathbf{A}}\otimes\mathrm{I})))\text{vec}(E_{t})+\text{vec}(r_{t+1})=F_{t+1}\text{vec}(E_{t})+\text{vec}(r_{t+1})\,.

Clearly, the matrix
(\mathrm{I}\otimes\bar{\mathbf{A}})+(\bar{\mathbf{A}}\otimes\mathrm{I})
is Hurwitz. Therefore, due to the
13
, we have

\|F_{t}\|\leq\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}(1-\eta Hb)^{1/2}\,,\quad\eta_{t}H_{t}\leq\beta_{\infty}\,.

Now, to bound
R_{t}
, we apply
Lemma

22
, thus

\displaystyle\|R_{t}\|\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\eta_{t}^{2}H_{t}^{2}\,.

Therefore, using
Lemma

13
and the bound
\|G^{\sf avg}_{t}-\mathrm{I}\|\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\eta_{t}H_{t}
, the remainder term
r_{t}
can be bounded, as

\displaystyle\|r_{t+1}\|\leq 8\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}\gamma_{\eta}(1+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(\eta H)^{2})(1+t)^{-1}+2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}(\eta_{t+1}H_{t+1})^{2}\,.

Using that
\|\text{vec}(r_{t+1})\|\leq d^{1/2}\|r_{t+1}\|
, we get

\displaystyle\|\text{vec}(E_{t+1})\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}\prod_{i=1}^{t+1}(1-\eta_{i}H_{i}b)^{1/2}\|\text{vec}(\hat{\Sigma}_{1}-\Sigma_{\infty})\|+\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}d^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{t+1}\prod_{i=j+1}^{t+1}(1-\eta_{i}H_{i}b)^{1/2}\|r_{j}\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}\exp{\left(-{b\over 2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{t+1}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\|\text{vec}(\hat{\Sigma}_{1}-\Sigma_{\infty})\|

\displaystyle+16\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}\gamma_{\eta}(1+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(\eta H)^{2})d^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{t+1}(1+j)^{-1}\exp{\left(-{b\over 2}\sum_{\ell=j+1}^{t+1}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle+2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}d^{1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{t+1}\eta_{j}^{2}H_{j}^{2}\exp{\left(-{b\over 2}\sum_{\ell=j+1}^{t+1}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}\exp{\left(-{b\over 2}\sum_{\ell=1}^{t+1}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\|\text{vec}(\hat{\Sigma}_{1}-\Sigma_{\infty})\|+M_{\Sigma,1}(2+t)^{\gamma-1}+M_{\Sigma,2}(2+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

where

\displaystyle M_{\Sigma,1}

\displaystyle=16\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}\gamma_{\eta}(1+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(\eta H)^{2})(1+2b^{-1}L(1+((b/2)\eta H)^{1+\gamma\over 1-\gamma}))d^{1/2}\,,

\displaystyle M_{\Sigma,2}

\displaystyle=8\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}((\eta H)^{2}+b^{-1}L\eta H(1+((b/2)\eta H)^{3\gamma\over 1-\gamma}))d^{1/2}\,.

Finally, using (
105
), (
106
), the bound
xe^{-cx}\leq(c\mathrm{e})^{-1}
and that
\|E_{t}\|\leq\|E_{t}\|_{F}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{\infty}\|\leq\|\Sigma_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\|+\|\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\Sigma_{\infty}\|\leq C_{\infty,1}(1+t)^{\gamma-1}+C_{\infty,2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

where we have used that
\|\text{vec}(\Sigma_{\infty})\|\leq(2\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathbf{A}}))^{-1}N^{-1}\|\text{vec}(\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg})\|
, and have set

\displaystyle C_{\infty,1}

\displaystyle=\kappa_{Q}^{1/2}\left({b\mathrm{e}\over 2(1-\gamma)}\eta H\right)^{-1}e^{{b\over 1-\gamma}\eta H}d^{1/2}(2\eta H+(2\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathbf{A}}))^{-1})N^{-1}\|\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}\|+M_{\Sigma,1}\,,

(99)

\displaystyle C_{\infty,2}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{\Sigma,2}+2(\operatorname{c}_{M,3}^{2}+\operatorname{c}_{M,4}^{2})\,.

(100)

∎

Lemma 12
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
(2),

2
,

3
, and
4
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}^{-1/2}\|\leq N^{1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}\,,

(101)

where
\Sigma_{t}
is defined in (
65
) and

\displaystyle\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma}}=\sqrt{2}(\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}))^{-1/2}\,.

Proof.

Applying Lidski’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{t})\geq\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})-\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{\infty}\|

Thus, under
4
, we get that

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{\infty}\|\leq 2\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathbf{A}}){\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})\over 2}\,.

Finally, using the relation
\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})\leq\|\Sigma_{\infty}\|\leq(2\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathbf{A}}))^{-1}N^{-1}\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg})
, the result (
101
) follows.
∎

Proposition 6
.

Under assumptions
1
,
2
and
4
, with probability at least
1-2/t
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\lesssim_{\log_{t}}N^{-3/2}t^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}+N^{-1}t^{-2\gamma}\,,

(102)

where
\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}
is defined in (
65
). Moreover, on the event (
102
), under (assumption on t), we have

\displaystyle\|(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})^{-1/2}\|\leq N^{1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}\,,

and

\displaystyle\operatorname{C}_{{\bf\Sigma^{\mathsf{b}}}}=2(\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{*}^{\sf avg}))^{-1/2}\,.

Proof.

Firstly, we note that

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\leq\|\Sigma_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\|+\|\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|+\|\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\,,

where
\hat{\Sigma}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\hat{M}_{t}\hat{M}_{t}^{T}]
and
\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}=\mathbb{E}^{\mathsf{b}}[M_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}M_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}})^{T}]
. Then, we note

\displaystyle\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}=N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\underbrace{\eta_{s}^{2}\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}\{\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})(\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c}))^{T}-\Sigma^{c}_{*}\}(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i})^{T}}_{U_{s,h,c}}\,.

We note that
\mathbb{E}[U_{s,h,c}]=0
and it can be bounded, as

\displaystyle\|U_{s,h,c}\|\leq\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{2}+\|\Sigma^{c}_{*}\|)\leq U_{\max}\eta_{t}^{2}\,.

where we have set

\displaystyle U_{\max}=\left(9+\left({2\gamma_{\eta}\over 2a\eta H\mathrm{e}}\right)^{2\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}\exp{\left({6a\eta H\over 1-\gamma}\right)}\right)(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{2}+\max_{c\in[N]}\|\Sigma^{c}_{*}\|)

Hence, applying
Lemma

25
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\sum_{s,h,c}\mathbb{E}[U_{s,h,c}U_{s,h,c}^{T}]\|\leq 2N\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}\exp{\left(-4a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{4}+\sigma_{*}^{2})\leq\operatorname{c}_{U}N\eta_{t}^{3}\,,

where

\operatorname{c}_{U}=4(\eta H+a^{-1}L(1+(4a\eta H)^{\gamma+3\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}^{4}+\sigma_{*}^{2})

Hence, we can apply the matrix Bernstein inequality
52
, which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\|\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\geq x)=\mathbb{P}(\|\sum_{s,h,c}U_{s,h,c}\|\geq N^{2}\eta_{t}x)\leq 2d\exp{\left(-{N^{3}x^{2}\over\eta_{t}(2\operatorname{c}_{U}+2U_{\max}Nx/3)}\right)}\,.

Equivalently, with probability at least
1-\delta
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Sigma}_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\leq\left({2\operatorname{c}_{U}\eta\over N^{3}}\right)^{1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\sqrt{\log{(2d/\delta)}}+{U_{\max}\eta\over 3N^{2}}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}}\log{(2d/\delta)}\,.

(103)

Combining the results (
103
), (
104
) and (
107
) with
\delta=1/t
, we obtain (
102
).

In order to prove the second part of the lemma, we apply Lidski’s inequality, and get

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}})\geq\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{t})-\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\geq\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})/4\,.

Then, by
4
, we choose
n_{0}
such that for
t\geq n_{0}
, we have
\|\Sigma_{t}-\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\leq\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})/4
. Also, under this assumption, we have
\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{t})\geq\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\infty})/2
as stated in
Lemma

12
.
∎

Lemma 13
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
(
\log(t)
),

2
,

3
, and
4
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|M_{t}\|^{2}]\leq\operatorname{c}_{M}N^{-1}\,.

Particularly, we get that

\displaystyle\|\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\|=\|\mathbb{E}[\hat{M}_{t}\hat{M}_{t}^{T}]\|\leq\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}N^{-1}\,,

where
\hat{M}_{t}=M_{t,1}+M_{t,2}
, and

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}\|\leq(\operatorname{c}_{M,3}^{2}+\operatorname{c}_{M,4}^{2})N^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}\,.

(104)

Proof.

Applying again
Lemma

22
and
Lemma

25
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|M_{t,3}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|(G_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c}-\mathrm{I})\varepsilon^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\|^{2}]\exp{\Big(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{3}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq 4\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}^{2}\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}((\eta H)^{3}+a^{-1}L(\eta H)^{2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{-{3\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}}))N^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{M,3}^{2}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}\,,

(105)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|M_{t,4}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq N^{-2}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}[\|(G_{s,1:h-1}^{c}-\mathrm{I})\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})(\theta_{\star}-\theta^{c}_{\star})\|^{2}]\exp{\Big(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq 4\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{het}}^{2}((\eta H)^{3}+a^{-1}L(\eta H)^{2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{-{3\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}}))N^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{c}_{M,4}^{2}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}\,.

(106)

Similarly, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|M_{t,1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{\sigma}_{\varepsilon}^{2}(\eta H+a^{-1}L(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))N^{-1}=\operatorname{c}_{M,1}N^{-1}\,,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|M_{t,2}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{het}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}H_{s}\exp{\Big(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\Big)}

\displaystyle\leq 2\bar{\sigma}_{\textrm{het}}^{2}(\eta H+a^{-1}L(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 1-\gamma}))N^{-1}=\operatorname{c}_{M,2}N^{-1}\,.

Finally, setting
\operatorname{c}_{M}=\operatorname{c}_{M,1}+\operatorname{c}_{M,2}+\operatorname{c}_{M,3}+\operatorname{c}_{M,4}
and
\operatorname{c}_{\hat{\Sigma}}=\operatorname{c}_{M,1}+\operatorname{c}_{M,2}
, we get the result.
∎

Lemma 14
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
2
(
\log(t)
),

2
,

3
, and
4
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq 2^{13}\operatorname{c}_{M,2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}N^{-2}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}}+2^{8}\operatorname{c}_{M,3}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{3}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\gamma}\,,

where
\hat{\Sigma}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[M_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}}(M_{t,1}^{\mathsf{b}})^{T}]
. Moreover, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|\lesssim\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{M,2}^{\mathsf{b}}N^{-2}\log^{6}(1/\delta)(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}}+\bar{\operatorname{c}}_{M,3}^{\mathsf{b}}N^{-1}\log^{3}(1/\delta)(1+t)^{-2\gamma}\,.

(107)

Proof.

We note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Sigma_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}-\hat{\Sigma}_{t}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]\leq\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|(M_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}+M_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}})\|^{2p}]\leq 2\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|M_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{2p}]+2\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|M_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{2p}]\,.

Now, applying Burkholder’s inequality to the sequence
\{(\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i})(w_{s,h,c}-1)\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})\}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|M_{t,2}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p^{3}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(w_{s,h,c}-1)\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq 4a^{-1}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{6}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{3}H_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{M,2}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{6}N^{-1}(1+t)^{-\gamma_{\eta}/2}\,,

where

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{M,2}^{\mathsf{b}}=8a^{-1}W_{\max}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}(\eta H^{1/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}\eta^{1/2}(1+(2a\eta H)^{\gamma+2\gamma\over 2(1-\gamma)}))(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})\,,

and we used that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\leq 2a^{-1/2}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}N^{-1/2}\eta_{s}^{1/2}\exp{\left(-a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\,.

Again, applying Burkholder’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}

\displaystyle[\|M_{t,3}^{\mathsf{b}}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p^{3}N^{-1}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{2}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{s}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(w_{s,h,c}-1)(\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,h+1:H_{s}}^{c})\varepsilon^{c}(\theta_{\star},Z_{s,h,c})\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}p^{3}N^{-1/2}\eta_{t}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{s=1}^{t}\eta_{s}^{4}H_{s}^{3}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\right)^{1/2}(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{c}_{M,3}^{\mathsf{b}}p^{3}N^{-1/2}(1+t)^{-\gamma}\,,

where

\displaystyle\operatorname{c}_{M,3}^{\mathsf{b}}=2\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}W_{\max}((\eta H)^{3/2}+a^{-1/2}L^{1/2}\eta H(1+(2a\eta H)^{3\gamma+\gamma_{\eta}\over 2(1-\gamma)}))(\|\varepsilon\|_{{\infty}}+\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\zeta_{3})\,,

and we also used that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\mathrm{I}-\Gamma_{s,h-1}^{c}\|^{p}]\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}\eta_{s}H_{s}\,.

To prove (
107
), we apply Markov’s inequality with
p=\log(t)
.
∎

Lemma 15
.

Assume
1
(2),
2
and
2
. For any
k\neq c
and
n\neq t
, we have
\Gamma_{n,\alpha:\beta}^{k}-\Gamma_{n,\alpha:\beta}^{k,(t,j,c)}=0
. For the case
k=c
and
n=t
, we obtain the bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c,(t,j,c)}\|^{2}]\leq 4\eta_{t}\mathrm{e}^{-a\eta_{t}(\beta-\alpha+1)}\sqrt{\sf{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})},

where
1\leq\alpha\leq\beta\leq H_{n}
.

Proof.

Decompose
\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c,(t,j,c)}
using
Lemma

22

\displaystyle\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c,(t,j,c)}=

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{i=\alpha}^{\beta}\left\{\prod_{\ell=\alpha}^{i-1}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,\ell}^{c}))\right\}\eta_{t}\left(\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,i,c}^{(t,j,c)})-\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,i,c})\right)\left\{\prod_{\ell=i+1}^{\beta}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,\ell,c}^{(t,j,c)}))\right\}

For any
i\neq j
the corresponding summand is zero. Therefore

\displaystyle\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c,(t,j,c)}=\left\{\prod_{\ell=\alpha}^{j-1}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,\ell}^{c}))\right\}\eta_{t}\left(\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c}^{(t,j,c)})-\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c})\right)\left\{\prod_{\ell=j+1}^{\beta}(\mathrm{I}-\eta_{t}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,\ell,c}^{(t,j,c)}))\right\}

Using assumption
1
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c,(t,j,c)}\|^{2}]\leq\eta_{t}(1-\eta_{t}a)^{\beta-\alpha}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c}^{(t,j,c)})-\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c})\|^{2}]

As
\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c}^{(t,j,c)})-\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c})=\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c}^{(t,j,c)})-\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c})
then, using Minkowski’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c}-\Gamma_{t,\alpha:\beta}^{c,(t,j,c)}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{1-\eta_{t}a}\eta_{t}(1-\eta_{t}a)^{\beta-\alpha+1}\mathbb{E}^{1/2}[\|\widetilde{\mathbf{A}}^{c}(Z_{t,j,c})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{t}\mathrm{e}^{-a\eta_{t}(\beta-\alpha+1)}\sqrt{\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})}

∎

Lemma 16
.

Under assumptions
1
(2),
2
and
2
with
\gamma_{\eta}\geq\gamma_{H}
, for any
s\in\{0,\dots,t-1\}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\,.

Proof.

Using
Lemma

22
, we also note that the sequence
\{\prod\limits_{j=s+1}^{i-1}G^{\sf avg}_{j}(G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i})\prod\limits_{j=i}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{j},s+1\leq i\leq t\}
is martingale-difference, thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{j=s+1}^{i-1}G^{\sf avg}_{j}(G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i})\prod_{j=i}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{j}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\exp{(2a\eta_{i}H_{i})}\mathbb{E}[\|G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]\,.

Again, applying
Lemma

22
to the last term, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq N^{-2}\eta_{i}^{2}\sum_{c=1}^{N}\sum_{h=1}^{H_{i}}\exp{(-2a\eta_{i}(H_{i}-h-1))}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{i}^{2}H_{i}\,.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}G^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}N^{-1}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}^{2}H_{i}

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{-1}\bar{\sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{2}N^{-1}\eta_{s}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\,,

where we used the inequality
k\exp{(-2ck)}\leq(c\mathrm{e})^{-1}\exp{(-ck)}
.
∎

Lemma 17
.

Under assumptions
1
(2),
2
and
2
with
\gamma_{\eta}\geq\gamma_{H}
, for any
s\in\{1,\dots,t\},h\in\{1,\dots,H_{s}\},c\in\{1,\dots,N\}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}\|^{2}]\leq 16N^{-2}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\,.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\Delta^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Delta^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=N^{-2}\mathbb{E}[\|(\Gamma_{s}^{c,(s,h,c)}-\Gamma_{s}^{c})(\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq 16N^{-2}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\|\theta^{c}_{\star}-\theta_{\star}\|^{2}\,.

where we have applied
Lemma

15
.
∎

Lemma 18
.

Under assumptions
1
(2),
2
and
2
with
\gamma_{\eta}\geq\gamma_{H}
, for any
s\in\{1,\dots,t\},h\in\{1,\dots,H_{s}\},c\in\{1,\dots,N\}
and
s^{\prime}<s
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\|^{2}]\leq 4\mathrm{e}N^{-2}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,.

Proof.

Using Minkowski’s inequality and
Lemma

22
, we note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\|^{2}]\leq\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{s-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s})\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\|^{2}]\,,

since
\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}=0
for
i\neq s
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{i}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}\|^{2}]\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mathrm{e}N^{-2}\eta_{s}^{2}\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s^{\prime}+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,.

where we have used that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{s}-\Gamma^{\sf{avg},(s,h,c)}_{s}\|^{2}]=N^{-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\Gamma_{s,H_{s}}^{c}-\Gamma_{s,H_{s}}^{c,(s,h,c)}\|^{2}]\leq 4N^{-2}\eta_{s}^{2}\mathrm{Tr}(\Sigma^{c}_{\mathbf{A}})\,.

∎

Lemma 19
.

Under the assumptions
1
(2),
2
and
2
, for any
1\leq i\leq t
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}m_{p}p^{2}N^{-1/2}\eta_{i}H_{i}^{1/2}\,.

Proof.

Applying Burkholder’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq pN^{-1}\left(\sum_{c=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\Gamma_{i}^{c}-\Gamma_{i}^{\mathsf{b},c}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}m_{p}p^{2}N^{-1/2}\eta_{i}H_{i}^{1/2}\,.

where, using the martingale-difference structure of the sequence
\{(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{i,1:h-1}^{c}\mathbf{A}^{z}(Z_{s,h,c})\Gamma_{i,h+1:H_{i}}^{c},1\leq h\leq H_{i}\}
w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{i,h}^{\mathsf{b}}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\Gamma_{i}^{c}-\Gamma_{i}^{\mathsf{b},c}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\eta_{i}\left(\sum_{h=1}^{H_{i}}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|(w_{s,h,c}-1)\Gamma_{i,1:h-1}^{c}\mathbf{A}^{c}(Z_{s,h,c})\Gamma_{i,h+1:H_{i}}^{\mathsf{b},c}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}m_{p}p\eta_{i}H_{i}^{1/2}\,.

∎

Lemma 20
.

Under the assumptions
1
(2),
2
and
2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\leq\mathrm{e}^{1/2}a^{-1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}m_{p}p^{3}N^{-1/2}\exp{\left(-{a\over 2}\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\,.

Proof.

Using
Lemma

22
, the fact that the sequence
\{\prod_{j=s+1}^{i-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i})\prod_{j=i}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i},s+1\leq i\leq t\}
it martingale-difference w.r.t filtration
\mathcal{F}_{s,h}^{\mathsf{b},+}
and applying Burkholder’s inequality, together with
Lemma

19
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{1/p}[\|\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\prod_{i=s+1}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq p\left(\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\prod_{j=s+1}^{i-1}\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}(\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i})\prod_{j=i}^{t}\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}p\left(\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\mathbb{E}^{2/p}[\|\Gamma^{\sf avg}_{i}-\Gamma^{\mathsf{b},\sf avg}_{i}\|^{p}]\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}m_{p}p^{3}N^{-1/2}\left(\exp{\left(-2a\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\sum_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}^{2}H_{i}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}^{1/2}a^{-1/2}\operatorname{C}_{{\bf A}}m_{p}p^{3}N^{-1/2}\exp{\left(-{a\over 2}\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}\eta_{\ell}H_{\ell}\right)}\,.

where we have used that
k\exp(-2ck)\leq(c\mathrm{e})^{-1}\exp(-ck)
.
∎

Below we state the Gaussian comparison inequality due to
11
, see also
2
.

Lemma 21
.

Let
\Sigma_{1}
and
\Sigma_{2}
be positive definite covariance matrices in
\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
. Let
X\sim\mathrm{N}(0,\Sigma_{1})
and
Y\sim\mathrm{N}(0,\Sigma_{2})
. Then

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{\mathsf{TV}}(X,Y)\leq{3\over 2}\|\Sigma_{2}^{-1/2}\Sigma_{1}\Sigma_{2}^{-1/2}-\mathrm{I}\|_{\mathsf{F}}\,.

Here we present some useful technical lemmas we use during the proofs of our main results.

Lemma 22
.

For any matrix-valued sequences
(A_{i})_{i=1}^{N}
and
(B_{i})_{i=1}^{N}
it holds that:

\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{N}A_{i}-\prod_{i=1}^{N}B_{i}=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\Big\{\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}A_{j}\Big\}(A_{i}-B_{i})\Big\{\prod_{j=i+1}^{N}B_{j}\Big\}

Lemma 23
.

Assume
\eta_{t}=c_{0}(t_{0}+t)^{-\gamma}
for some
\gamma\in[1/2,1)
. Then:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}\leq\frac{\eta}{1-\gamma}\left((1+t)^{1-\gamma}-(1+s)^{1-\gamma}\right)

Proof.

By definition of
\eta_{t}
:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta_{i}=\sum\limits_{i=s+1}^{t}\eta(1+i)^{-\gamma}=\eta\sum\limits_{k=s+2}^{1+t}k^{-\gamma}

As
f(x)=x^{-\gamma}
is a decreasing function for
x\geq 0
, then:

\displaystyle c_{0}\sum\limits_{k=s+2}^{1+t}k^{-\gamma}\leq\eta\int_{1+s}^{1+t}x^{-\gamma}\mathrm{d}x=\frac{\eta}{1-\gamma}((1+t)^{1-\gamma}-(1+s)^{1-\gamma})\,.

∎

Lemma 24
.

Assume
\gamma\in(0,1)
and
1\leq\ell\leq r\in\mathbb{N}
. Then,

\displaystyle\frac{(r+2)^{1-\gamma}-(\ell+1)^{1-\gamma}}{1-\gamma}\leq\sum\limits_{k=\ell}^{r}(1+k)^{-\gamma}\leq\frac{(r+1)^{1-\gamma}-\ell^{1-\gamma}}{1-\gamma}\,.

Proof.

As
f(x)=(1+x)^{-\gamma}
is a decreasing function, then

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{k=\ell}^{r}(1+k)^{-\gamma}\leq\int_{\ell-1}^{r}(1+x)^{-\gamma}\mathrm{d}x=\int_{\ell}^{r+1}x^{-\gamma}\mathrm{d}x=\frac{(r+1)^{1-\gamma}-\ell^{1-\gamma}}{1-\gamma}\,.

Similarly, we can give a lower bound of our function as,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{k=\ell}^{r}(1+k)^{-\gamma}\geq\int_{\ell}^{r+1}(1+x)^{-\gamma}\mathrm{d}x=\int_{\ell+1}^{r+2}(1+x)^{-\gamma}\mathrm{d}x=\frac{(r+2)^{1-\gamma}-(\ell+1)^{1-\gamma}}{1-\gamma}

∎

Lemma 25
.

For any
t\geq 1
and
\beta<1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t-1}s^{-\alpha}\exp{\{-u\sum_{\ell=s+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\beta}\}}\leq Lu^{-1}(1+u^{-{\alpha\over 1-\beta}})t^{\beta-\alpha}\,,

where
L
is defined in (
108
).

Proof.

We set
m=\lceil{t\over 2}\rceil
. To estimate

\displaystyle S_{t}:=\sum\limits_{s=1}^{t-1}s^{-\alpha}\exp{\left(-u\sum\limits_{\ell=s+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\beta}\right)}\,,

we split this sum into two parts:

\displaystyle S_{t}=\underbrace{\sum\limits_{s=1}^{m}s^{-\alpha}\exp{\left(-u\sum\limits_{\ell=s+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\beta}\right)}}_{\text{$S_{t,1}$}}+\underbrace{\sum\limits_{s=m+1}^{t}s^{-\alpha}\exp{\left(-u\sum\limits_{\ell=s+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\beta}\right)}}_{\text{$S_{t,2}$}}\,.

Estimating
S_{t,1}
. Here, we note that for
s\leq m
:

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{\ell=s+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\beta}\geq\sum\limits_{\ell=m+1}^{t}(1+\ell)^{-\beta}\geq\frac{t}{2}t^{-\beta}=\frac{1}{2}t^{1-\beta}\,.

Then

\displaystyle S_{t,1}\leq\exp{\left(-\frac{u}{2}t^{1-\beta}\right)}\sum\limits_{s=1}^{m}s^{-\alpha}\leq t\exp{\left(-\frac{u}{2}t^{1-\beta}\right)}\,.

We can see that the function
g(x)=x^{1+\alpha-\beta}\exp{(-vx^{1-\beta})}
for
x\geq 1
can be uniformly bounded by the value

\displaystyle L_{0}(v)=\left({1+\alpha-\beta\over v\mathrm{e}(1-\beta)}\right)^{1+\alpha-\beta\over 1-\beta}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{e}^{-1}\Big(1+{\alpha\over 1-\beta}\Big)^{1+{\alpha\over 1-\beta}}v^{-\left(1+{\alpha\over 1-\beta}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq L_{1}v^{-\left(1+{\alpha\over 1-\beta}\right)}\,,

where we have set
L_{1}=\mathrm{e}^{-1}\Big(1+{\alpha\over 1-\beta}\Big)^{1+{\alpha\over 1-\beta}}
. Therefore, we obtain the bound

\displaystyle S_{t,1}\leq L_{0}(u/2)t^{\beta-\alpha}\,.

Estimating
S_{t,2}
.
Here, we know that
s^{-\alpha}\leq(t/2)^{-\alpha}
. That is, we get

\displaystyle S_{t,2}\leq 2^{\alpha}t^{-\alpha}\sum_{s=m+1}^{t-1}\exp{(-u(t-s)(1+t)^{-\beta})}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\alpha}t^{-\alpha}e^{-u(1+t)^{-\beta}}\left(1-e^{-u(1+t)^{-\beta}}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\alpha+\beta}u^{-1}t^{\beta-\alpha}\,,

where we have used that
{1\over 1-e^{-x}}\leq{a\over 1-e^{-a}}x^{-1}
for any
x\leq a
, that is, in our case
a=2^{-\beta}u
. Finally, setting

\displaystyle L=2^{\alpha+\beta}+2^{1+2\alpha}L_{1}\,,

(108)

we obtain the result.
∎
